## What the evidence does and does not prove

- `frame-proof.json` and the PNG thumbnails prove that the **recompiled native binary** emitted content with more than one colour on this host. They are **not** a Windows oracle.
- `highball.log` records attempts to launch the original `.exe` under Wine/Highball. Timeouts and missing DLLs are negative results; they do not establish equivalence.
- A B1 `BUILD-ONLY` status stays BUILD-ONLY even if a later test run launched the binary: the run/verify stages of the pipeline were not executed.
- Dual-engine columns are fail-closed. `BLOCKED` means the engine was attempted and returned an exact reason (Unicorn cannot map many original entry states; live Frida on this macOS host returns `NotSupportedError: unexpected error while probing dyld of target process`, consistent with taskgated/SIP). `MATCH` from native replay alone is **not** upgraded to PASS when either differential engine is blocked.
- Cross-OS columns are source-level only. `tools/win32_crossbuild.py` (macOS→Windows) fails on POSIX `win32hle`/SDL2 headers for generated game projects; `tools/linux_to_windows.py` (PR #176) is BLOCKED with `NO_REAL_SOURCE` where no real C source exists, while PR #176's shared proof shows a real host C source building to PE32/PE32+ and printing stdout under Wine. Windows→Linux (PR #175) and x64→macOS (PR #174) are path-level citations, not per-target passes.
- No game binary, generated project tree or full-size frame is vendored into this repository; only small logs, JSON reports and downscaled PNG previews are copied.

## Reproduce

```sh
cp <b1-builds>/results.jsonl reports/b1_results_snapshot.jsonl
python3 tools/verify_corpus.py
python3 tools/run_corpus_verification.py
python3 tools/verify_corpus.py
python3 -m pytest tests/test_corpus_final_report.py -q
```

The full suite also passes 173 tests with 2 skips; one unrelated pre-existing failure (`tools/assets/test_bin2iso.py`, missing/broken `node` asset helper) is not caused by this report.
